- **ecdna-breakpoint-concordance-comparison**: Use when circlehunter has been run on ATAC-Seq samples (e.g. GBM39, PC3, COLO320DM) with known WGS- and FISH-validated ecDNA, and you must verify that predicted ligation relationships and breakpoints match the reference structures. - **ecdna-breakpoint-posterior-inference**: Use when ATAC-Seq reads already support a candidate ecDNA junction (discordant pairs with anomalous orientation/insert size plus soft-clipped/supplementary-aligned pileups) and you need an exact breakpoint MAP estimate with a 95% CI, without full read realignment. - **ecdna-candidate-region-filtering**: Use when low-coverage WGS of tumor samples has been aligned (e.g., BWA to hg19) and focal amplification candidates must be narrowed before AmpliconArchitect eccDNA reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/ecdna-candidate-region-filtering))
- **ecdna-detection-from-atacseq**: Use when ATAC-Seq libraries from tumor/cancer samples must be scanned for ecDNA (focal circular amplifications, often &gt;1 Mb carrying oncogenes like MYC, MYCN, EGFR) without WGS, or when comparing ecDNA callers like circlehunter vs Circle_finder. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ecdna-detection-from-atacseq))
- **ecdna-gene-annotation**: Use when you have ecDNA interval calls (e.g., circlehunter Snakemake output) over tumor ATAC-Seq samples and need to count amplified genes, flag known oncogenes, and test their functional enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ecdna-gene-annotation))
- **ecdna-genome-segment-simulation**: Use when benchmarking ecDNA-calling tools on ATAC-Seq data and you need simulated ground-truth ecDNA amplicons spanning controlled local depths (e.g. 10×–30×+) and read lengths (e.g. 35–150 bp). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ecdna-genome-segment-simulation))
- **ecdna-size-chromosome-distribution-profiling**: Use when you have per-sample ecDNA calls from tumor ATAC-Seq (e.g., circlehunter predictions over hundreds of GEO samples) and need cohort-level counts, ecDNA-positive sample percentages, and size/chromosome/segment distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ecdna-size-chromosome-distribution-profiling))
- **ecdomainminer-biosynthetic-domain-mining**: Use when building or rebuilding a curated pHMM feature library restricted to biosynthetic protein families (e.g., for BiG-SLiCE feature set 1) from a full Pfam release plus antiSMASH domain models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ecdomainminer-biosynthetic-domain-mining))
- **ecdsa**: Comprehensive guide to ecdsa. Master the concepts, implementation, best practices, and real-world applications of ecdsa in professional environments. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/security/ecdsa) · ★1)
